ሥጋሁ ፣ ለ8ቅዱስ ፡ ጠቢብ ፣ ወሰማዕት ፣ ዘስሙ ፡ አንያኖስ ።= ወወሀብዎሙ ፣ ብሩ ረ ፤ ብዙኃ ፡ ወነሥአዎ ፡ እምሐራ ፡ ወአብጽሕቻ ፡ ኅበ ቤቶሙ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፣ ውስ ተ ፡ ሣጹን ፡ ዘእብን ፡ ክቡር ፣ ወሰቀሉ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ማኅቶተ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ያበርህ ፣ ወት ረ ፤ ወአስተርአየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ ዓበይት ። ወቅዱሳንሰ ፡ ሐለዩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ማኅለቅቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፣ ኃላፊ ፣ ወበእንተ ፣ ትፍሥሕተ ፣ መንግሥተ ፣ ሰማያ ት። ወተሰነአዉ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወተአምኑ ፡ በስሙ ፣ ለአግዚ እነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንንዎሙ ፡ ወቀሠፍዎሙ ፡ በጥብጣቤ ፡ ዘአልህምት ፡ እስከ ፣ ውሕዘ ፣፡ ደሞሙ፣ ዲበ፣ምድር ። ወእምዝ ፣ ቀነዉ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡፣ በቅንዋተ ፣ ሐጺን ፡፣ ነዊሀት ፡ ወአንደ
holy sage and martyr, named Anyanos. They gave the soldiers much money and took from them the body of the Saint, which they carried to their house and which they placed in a reliquary adorned with precious stones. Before it they suspended a torch that was always to burn. Great miracles occurred from it. The saints, considering the end of this perishable world and considering the joys of the kingdom of heaven, by common accord, went to the city of Alexandria to confess the name of Our Lord Jesus Christ before the prefect of the city of Alexandria. The latter ordered them to be subjected to tortures; they were whipped with ox-hide thongs, until their blood flowed to the ground. Then, they pierced their bodies with long iron nails, and under them they lit a fire.
ዱ።፤መትሕቴሆሙ ፡አሳተ። ወፈነወ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ መልአኮ ፡ ወአድኃኖሙ ፡፣ እምውስተ ፣ እሳት =፡ ወእ ምድኅረዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፣፡ ይስቅልዎሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኦም ፡ ልዕልት ፡ እንዘ ፡ እገ ሪሆሙ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ወአርእስቲሆሙ ፡ ቀሩልቀሊተ ፣ ወሰቀልዎሙ ፡ እስከ ፣ ውሕዘ ፤ ደሞ ሙ ፡ እምአፉሆሙ ፡ ወእምአንፎሙ ፡ ወወረደ ፡ መልአከ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወአድኃ
God sent his angel who delivered them from this fire. Then the prefect ordered them to be suspended from a high tree, in such a way that they had their feet up and their heads down; they suspended them until blood flowed from their mouths and noses. The angel of God descended and delivered them from the suspension. The prefect ordered them to be chained; then they were sent to Farama. When they were

ኖሙ ፡-እምስቅለት ፡ ወአዘዘ ፣ መኩንን ፣ ከመ ፣ ይሞቅሕዎሙ ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ፈነ ውዎሙ ፡ ኀበ ፣ ፈርማ ፡=፡ ወዕበ ፣ ቆሙ ፡ "ቅድሜሁ ፡ ለመኩንን ፡ አንከረ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ እምጽንዖሙ ፡ ወእምሠናይ ፤ ምግባሪሆሙ ፡፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡፤ መኩንን ፡ ውግሩ ፡ ዕጣነ ፣ ለአማልክት ፡ ወኢሰምፁ ፡ ትእዛዞ ፣ ወዓበይዎ ።፡ ወደመረ ፡ ፄወ ፡ ምስለ ፣ ግንፋል፣ ወፍልፍል ፤ ወብሂእ ፡ ወከዓወ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ኤንፎሙ ፣ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ይትዔገሠ ፡ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ኩነኔ =፡ እምድኅረዝ ፣፡ ሐረውዎሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዓራተ ፡ ሐዩን ፡ ወአንደዱ ፡ መትሕ ቴሆሙ ፡፤ እሳተ ፡ ወመልሑ ፡ ጽፍራተ ፣፤ ኤደዊሆሙ ፡፣ ወእገሪሆሙ ፣ ወቀሠፍዎሙ ፣ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በድንቡሳት ፣ ዘሐን ፣ ውስተ ፣ አፉሆሙ ፡ ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፣ ሞተት ፣ ብእሲቱ ፡ ለመኩንን ፣፤ ወሰአሎሙ ፣ ከመ ፣ ይምሐርዎ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ኩነኔ ፡ ዘኩነኖሙ ፣ ወያንሥኡ ፣ ሎቱ ፣ ብእሲቶ ፡፣ እሞት ። ወሰአልዎ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ለእግ ዚእነ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወአንሥእዋ ፣ ለብእሷተ ፣ መኩንን ፡ እሞት ፡፡ ወአምነ-፣መኩን ን ፣ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፣፡ ዚአሁ ፣ በእግዚእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወሰደዶሙ ፣ ለቅ ዱሳን ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገምኑዲ ፣ ሀገሮሙ ። ወወሀቡ ፡ ዘተርፈ ፡ እምንዋዮሙ ፡ ለነ ዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡ ወወሀቡ ፣፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አንያኖስ ፡ ለ8ብእሲ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ዘስ ሙ ፡ ሰረባሞን ፡=፡ ወአዘዝዎ ፡ ከመ ፣ ያኅቱ ። ቅድሜሁ ፣ ማኅቶተ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ከመ፡ቀ
in the presence of the prefect, the latter was very astonished at their firmness and the beauty of their actions; he said to them: "Throw incense to the gods." They did not listen to his orders and refused. He mixed salt with brick dust, pepper, and vinegar, and poured the whole into their noses. They endured with patience all these tortures. Then, they were nailed to an iron bed; they lit a fire under them; they tore out the nails of their hands and feet; they inflicted upon them harsh floggings with clubs, equipped with spikes at the end. At this moment, the wife of the prefect died; he (P, O: the emperor) asked them to have pity on him, because of the tortures he had inflicted upon them, and to resurrect his dead wife; the Saints prayed to Christ, Our Lord, and resurrected the wife of the prefect. The latter and all those of his house believed in Our Lord Jesus Christ. The prefect sent the saints away. They went to Gamnudi, their city. They gave the rest of their goods to the poor and the unfortunate. They entrusted the body of Saint Anyanos to a just man, named Sarabamon (Sarapammon). They recommended that he light a light before him, as before. Then, they went to the city of Baramon (Barmun, Baramun) to confess their faith in Our Lord Jesus Christ before the prefect. The latter gave the order to inflict a great


[p. 279] {#po.7.p279}
ዳሚ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ኀበ ፣ ሀገረ ፡ በረሞን ፣ ወተአመኑ ፣ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክ ርስቶስ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ መኩንን ፣ ወአዘዘ ፣ ከመ ፣ ይቅሥፍዎሙ ፣ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወሰሐብዎሙ ፡፣ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሀገር ።= ወኮነ ፣ ደሞሙ ፣ ይውኅዝ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር፣ ወመጽአት ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ብህምት ፣ ወጽምምት ፡፣ ወነሥአት ፣ እምደሞሙ ፡ ወቀብዓት ፡ አፉሃ ፡ ወእዝና ፣ ወልባ ፤ ወሰምዓት ፡ ወተናገረት ፡ በጊዜሃ ፣ ወአዘዘ ፣ መኩንን ፡ ከ መ ፣ ይሞቅሕዎሙ ፣ ወሞቅሕዎሙ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ እምኩንኖቶሙ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከ መ፡ይምትሩ ፤ ርእሶሙ ፣ ወሀሎ ፡ ሰረባሞን ፤ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ በህየ ፣ ወመተሩ ፡ አርእስ ቲሆሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወነሥአ ፣ ሰረ ባሞን ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡፡ ወሀለው ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ካልኣን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ገም ኑዲ ፡ ወገነዝዎሙ ፡ በሰንይናት ፣ ወአልባሳት ፡፣ ክቡራት ፡ ወቀብእዎሙ ፡ በፅፍረታት ፡ ዘምዑዝ ፡ ጴናሁ ፡ ወጾርዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገሮሙ ፡ ገምኑዲ ።፡፡ ወሶበ ፣ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አ ፍኦ ፡ ሀገር ፤ ቆሙ ፡ እንስሳ ፤ እለ ፤ ይስሕቡ ፣ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ዲበ ፣ ሰረገላ ፣ ወኢያንሶ ሰው ፣ ወዘበጥዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይሖሩ ፣ ወኢያንሶሰው ። ወሰምዑ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፣፤ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘኃረዮ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንበር ፣ ሥጋነ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፤ ወአንበርዎሙ ፡ በህየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሐነጹ ፣ ሎሙ ፡፣ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወአንበሩ ፤ ሥጋሆ ሙ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፤ አንያኖስ ፡ ጠቢብ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ እስከ፣ ይእዜ ፡ በሀገረ ፣ ገምኑዲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ተኣምራት ፣ወመንክራት ፤
flogging upon them. They were led through the whole city; their blood flowed to the ground. A deaf and mute woman came to take some of their blood; she anointed her mouth, her ear, and her heart with it; immediately she spoke and heard. The prefect ordered them to be chained; they were chained. When he was tired of torturing them, he gave the order to cut off their heads; with them was Sarabamon in this place. They cut off their heads with the sword; they received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. Sarabamon took the bodies of the Saints. Other citizens of the city of Gamnudi were with him; they buried the bodies in precious shrouds and linens; they anointed them with fragrant ointments. Then they carried them to their city of Gamnudi. When they arrived outside the city, the beasts that were pulling the bodies, placed on a chariot, remained in place without moving; they struck them to make them advance, but they did not move. A voice was heard saying: "This is the place chosen by God, so that our bodies may dwell there." They left their bodies there, until they had built a church for them. In this church their bodies were placed, with the body of Saint Anyanos, the sage (the physician). They are still now in the city of Gamnudi, and perform wonders and miracles in great number. Saint Aberoh (Pireou) was very red-haired and of tall stature: the pupil of his eyes was dark blue like indigo. Saint


[p. 280] {#po.7.p280}
ብዙኃት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አቤሮኅ ፣ ቀይሕ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡፤ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወነዊኅ ፡ ቆሙ ፡ ወ ብንተ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ አዝረቅ ፣ አምሳለ ። ኒል ፡= ወቅዱስ ፡-አቶም ፡ ኮነ ፡ ነዊኃ ፣፡ቆ ሙ ፡ ወብንተ ፡ አፅይንቲሁ ፡ አምሳለ ፣ ኩሕል ፡ ወጸሊም ፡ ጽሕሙ ፡፤ ወተካየዶሙ ፡እ ግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ በጊዜ ፤ ምንዳቤሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዝይዜከር ፣ ስሞ ሙ ፡ አው ፡ ዘይገብር ፡፤ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ አምጣነ ፡ ክሂሎቱ ፡ ይደመስስ ፡ ኃጣው ኢሁ ፡ ወኢይሬኢ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ግሙራ ። እማዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለዓለ
Atom was of tall stature; the pupil of his eyes was black like antimony kohl; his beard was black. Our Lord Jesus Christ, at the time of their trials, promised that whoever would make mention of their names or celebrate their anniversary, to the extent that he could, would have his sins erased and would never see the torture. May God have mercy on us, through their intercession, forever and ever! Amen.
መ፣ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡= ሰላም ፡ ለአቤሮኅ ፡ ወለእ'ኑሁ ፡ አቶም ፡፡ በእንተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘሐሙ ፡ በጥብጣቤ ፡ ላሀም ፡፡ በተኣምሪሁ ፡ ዕፁብ ፡ ወመንክራቲሁ ፡ መድምም ፤ ዘሰሐቦሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ወአብጽሖሙ ፣ ለደም ፡፡፡
[Salutation to Aberoh (Pireou) and to his brother Atom, who, because of Christ, were flogged with ox-hide thongs. Salutation, salutation to Anyanos the martyr, who, by his admirable wonders and his astonishing miracles, drew them, Pireou and Atom, and made them attain the blood of martyrdom.]
አንያኖስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ሰላም ፡ ሰላም ።፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ብላኔ ፣ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ በራ ፡ ዘእምደወለ ፡ ሰካ ፣ እምደቡበ ፡ ግብጽ ፤ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቀሲስ ፣ ወሶበ ፣ ሰ”"ም ዓ ፡ ምንዳቤሆሙ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፣ ወቅትለቶሙ ፡ ለሰማዕታት ፣ ወወሀበ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ን ዋዮ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን = ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ሀገረ ፣ አንዴናው ፣ ወተአመነ ፡ በስ ሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ መኩንን ፤ ወኩነኖ ፣ ኩነኔ ፣ ዐቢየ ፣ ዘዘ ፣ ዚአሁ ፡ ኩነ
On this day also, Saint Abba Belane (Apollon) was martyred, from the city of Bara, in the district of Saka, in the north of Egypt; this saint was a priest. When he learned of the sufferings of the faithful and the massacre of the martyrs, he gave all his goods to the poor and the unfortunate, and went to the city of Andenaw (Antinoe). He confessed his faith in the name of Our Lord before the prefect; the latter condemned him to all sorts of great tortures, during many


[p. 281] {#po.7.p281}
ኔ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋፅለ ፡ ወመጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፥፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፤ በመንግሥተ ፣ ሰማያት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓ ለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን =፡
days. The saint surrendered his soul into the hands of God and received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us, through his intercession, forever and ever! So be it.

[I say salutation to the blessed Belane, of beautiful ministry, priest at the altar, in the church: having learned of the exile of the faithful and the massacre of the just by violence, distributing his goods to the poor, he stood before the tribunal, intrepid for martyrdom.]
በዓውደ ፡ ምዙናን ፡ ቆመ ፡ ጥቡዓ ፡ ለስምዕ ፡ገ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፣ አባ ፣ ሚሳኤል ፡ ነዳይ ፡ ዘቀበሮ ፡ አባ ፣ ኪሮ ስ ፡ ወዜናሁሰ ፡ ዕበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ባስት ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወተአምኃ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወርእየ፣ በህየ ፡ ሥዕለ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ “ማርያም ፣ ወላዲተ ፣ አምላክ ፡ ወአውኃዘ ፡ አንብዓ ፣ እም አዕይንቲሁ ፡፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ኦአግዝእትየ ፡ ተዘከርኒ ፡ በጸሎትኪ ።፡ ወተናገረቶ ፡ ሥዕል ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ለኪሮስ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ፍኖትከክ ፡ ኢትኅልፍ ፡ እምዝየ ፡ ተመየጥ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅ ደርከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትዕቀብ ፡ አፅፅምተ ፡ አቡከ ፡፡ ወሰሚዖ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ ሰገደ ፡ ታሕተ ። ሥዕል ፡ ፪፻ስግደታተ ፡ ወሰምዓ ፡ ቃለ ፣ በነገረ ፣ እብራይስጢ ፡ ዘይብሎ ፡ የአክለከ ፡ እ ስመ ፡ ነግድ ፡ አንተ ፡ ተዘግበ ፡ ለከ ፣ ውስተ ፡፣ መዛግብተ ፣ ምሕረት ፡ ወሶበ ፣ ወፅ
[On this same day, died Abba Misa'el, the poor, who was buried by Abba Kiros. As for his story, here it is: When Abba Kiros went, by order of God, to Dabra Bast, he arrived at a convent of monks. He visited the church and saw there the image of Our Lady Mary, mother of God; his eyes shed tears; he said to her: "O Our Lady Mary! remember me, in your prayer." The image spoke to Kiros and said to him: "May your way be good; do not pass by here, do not go into this convent; return to your dwelling, to guard the bones of your father." Abba Kiros, upon hearing these words, made 700 genuflections before the image of the Virgin and heard a voice that said to him, in

እ ፡ እምሀየ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ መልክፁ ፡ ወአዳም ፡ ኩለንታሁ ፣ ተባሀሉ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ይመስል ፡ ዘመጽአ ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢመጽለወ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወጸልእዎ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወአብእዖዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ማኅደር ፡ ወረከበ ፡ በህየ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ነዳየ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ግዱፍ ፣ ውስ ተ፣፡ምድር ፡ ወየሐምም ፡ ሕማመ ፡ መስፈረስ ፣ ወጉንዱይ ፡ መዋዕል ፣ እምዘ ፡ አኃ ዞ ፡ ወአልጸቀ ፡ ለመዊት ፡፡ ወርእየ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፣፤ ሚካኤል ፡ መንገለ ፣ ትርዓሲሁ 0፡ ወገብርኤል ፡ በትርጋፁ ፡ ሩፋኤል ፡ በየማኑ ፡ ወሰዳክያል ፡ በፀጋሙ ፣ ወይኬልልዎ ፡ በ ክነፊሆሙ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይሬእዮሙ ፡ እምቋብእ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ ባ ሕቲቱ ። ተአምኅዎ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ባሕ ፡ አዛል ፡ ዘኢይፈርህ ፡ ሞተ ፡ በቅ ድመ ፡ ቀታልያን ፡ ወአንከረ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ትነብሩ ፡ ዝየ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ብነ ፡ ትእዛዝ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ንፅቀቦ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ነዳይ ፡፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡፣ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ማዕዜኑ ፡ ወይቤልቻ ፡ እስክ ፡ አመ ፡ ይኤዝዘነ ፡ እ ግዚእነ ፡ ለአዕርጎቱ ፣ ወተመይጠ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፣ ኀበ ፡ መነኮስ ። ወይቤሎ ፣፡ እስፍ
the Hebrew language: "That is enough; since you are a stranger to this convent, your devotion will be rewarded in the treasury of mercy." When he went out from there, the monks saw his beautiful appearance and saw how charming he was in everything: they said to one another: "This monk resembles the one who came from Egypt; his body is not wasted by fasting and prayer"; they hated him greatly and lodged him in a bad lodging. He found there a very poor man, lying on the ground and sick with the disease called "masfaras" with which he had been afflicted for a long time; he was on the point of dying. Abba Kiros saw that Saint Michael was standing near his head, Gabriel near his feet, Raphael at his right, and Sadakyal at his left. They surrounded him with their wings; no man saw them, except Abba Kiros alone. The angels greeted Misa'el and said to him: "Long live the courageous man who does not fear death before the executioners!" Abba Kiros was astonished and said to the angels: "Why do you stay here?" They answered: "We have received the order from God to watch over this poor man." - "And until when?" said Abba Kiros. They answered: "Until God orders us to make him ascend to heaven." Abba Kiros turned toward the monk Misa'el and said to him: "How many years have you lived in this city?" He said to him: "Sixty-five years." He

ንተ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ዘነበርከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፣ ሀገር ፡ ወይቤሎ ፣ ፳ወ፳ዓመተ ።፡ ወካፅ በ፣ይቤ ፣፤ አስፍንቱመ ፣ እምዘ ፣ ደወይከ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፣ ፳ዓመት ። ወይቤሎ ፣ አባ፣ ኪሮስ ፡ ለምንትኩኬ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ወመነኮሳት ፡ ዘኢይሔውጹክ ።፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አልቦ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትሬእየኒ ፣ ፲ወድዓመት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይሬኢ ፡ ገጾሙ ፡ ወኢይበልዕ ፡ እ ክሎሙ ፡ ወኢይሰቲ ፡ ማዮሙ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ ሀብኬ ፡ ንግረኒ ፡ መኑ ፡ አቡ ከ፣ወመኑ ፣ እምከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡየ ፡፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ኬልቄዴያን ፡ ወእምየኒ ፣ ወለተ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ራኅራኅ ፡ አማንየ ፡ እብለከ ፡ ኦአባ ፣ ኪሮስ ፡ ለወርቅ ፣፡ ወለብሩር ፡ ወለአልባ ሰ ፡ ቀጠንት ፡ ይከይድዎ ፡ በቤተ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ መኑኬ ፡ አብጽሐ ከ፣ ዝየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ምኔት ።፡ ወይቤሎ ፣ በጽሑ ፡ ፪ዕደው ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡየ › አለ ፡ ከ ማከ ፡ ወቤቱ ፡ ህየ ፡ ወጊዜ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ብአሲ ፡ ብሩህ ፡ ጸው ዓኒ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ሚሳኤል ፡ ሚሳኤል ፡ ሚሳኤል ፡፣ ጊዜ ፡ ነግህ ፡ ተንሥእኬ ፡ ሑር ፡ ም ስለ፣-እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ በዝኬ ፡ ግብር ፡ ዘበጻሕኩ ፣ ወኮንኩ ፣ ከማሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይገ ብር ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፣፡ ኪሮስ ፡ ስማዕ ፡ እስመ ፡፣ አነ ፡ እናዝዘከ = ወሀሎ፣ 8መነኮስ ፣ ዘስሙ ፡ በብኑዳ ፡ ነዳይ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ ዘንብረቱ ፡፣ ገዳም ፣፤ ወመጽአ ፡ ኀቤ ሁ ፡ ጸላዔ ፡ ሠናያት ፤ ወአስፈጦ ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሀበ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለአንበሳ ፡ ወለ
said to him again: "How many years have passed since you have been sick?" - "Twenty years," he answered him. Abba Kiros said to him: "Why do the superior of the convent and the monks not come to visit you?" He said: "Never, O my lord! As you see me, I have remained fifteen years, without seeing their faces, nor eating their food, nor drinking their water." Abba Kiros said to him: "Come! Tell me who is your father and who is your mother?" Misa'el answered: "My father is the king of Kelgedeyan (A: Kelgedonawi) and my mother is the daughter of the king of Rahrah; I tell you, in truth, O Abba Kiros, in the house of my father they trample gold, silver, and precious garments underfoot." Abba Kiros said to him: "Who then brought you here to this convent?" He said: "Two men like you came to my father, and there, in the house of my father, they spent the night. At midnight, a luminous man came to me, he called me and said to me: Misa'el, Misa'el, Misa'el! at the hour of the morning, rise and go with these saints, these monks. That is how I came to this place, and I was like them, without doing good." Abba Kiros said to him: "Listen, for I will console you. There was a very poor monk, who was called Babnuda (Paphnuce) and who lived in the desert. The enemy of good, the devil, came to him and seduced him; God gave to the lions and the birds of the heaven the body of this poor man of whom I speak to you. He too, he was the

አፅዋፈ ፡ ሰማይ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ ነዳይ ፣ ዘእቤለክ ፣ ውእቱኬ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፡ ኬፋዝ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ዘመድ ፡ ዘይመልክ ፡ እምታሕቴሁ ፡ ልንጉሠ ፡ ሮምያ ፡፡ ወካዕበ ፡ እነግረከ ፡ ሀ ለዉ 8ነዳይ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፡ ሮምያ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምቤቱ ፡ ድቡተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይነ ሥአ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ዘአንበለ ፡ በትር ፡ ባሕቲታ ፡ ወአንስ ፡ አአምሮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእ ሲ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ሀለወ ፡ የዓይል ፡ እምገዳም ። ውስተ ፡ ገጻም ፡ ወእምአድባር ፡ ውስተ ፡ አድባር ፡ ሞቶሂ ፤፡ ኢለበውኩ ፡ ወሕይወቶጄሂ ፡ ኢያእመርኩ ።፡ ወሀሎ ፡ ፳ጳነዳይ ፡ ወል ዱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይተግሀ ፡ መዓልተ ። ወሌሊተ ፡ በልብሰ ፡ ሠቅ ፡ ኢይበልዕ ፡ ወኢይሰቲ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በሰንበታት ፡ ወበዘከመዝ ፡ ግብር ፡ ነበረ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በክብር ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወጫምዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ይ እዜኒ ፡ ስማዕ ፡ ኦእጉየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንግርከ ፣ ኢታስተዓፅብ ፡ ተግሣጸ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈጣሪከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለዘአፍቀረ ፣ ይጌሥጽ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ ውኔቱ ፡ መነኮ ስ ፣ርቱዕ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ዘይደልወኒ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ኃጢአትየ ፡ ተፈደይኩ ፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ አርመመ ፡ ወበይእቲ ፣ ዕለት ፡ ጸለየ ፡ አባ ፣ ኪሮስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፣ይ ሥዓሮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ድውይ ፡ ወግብተ ፡ መጽአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎ ቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ፳ወአዕላፍ ፡ ወትእልፊተ ፡ አዕላፋት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴብሕዎ ፡ ወይዜምሩ ፡ ወይብሉ ፡ ሃሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡፤ ለአምላክነ ፡ ሃሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ለንጉሦሙ ፡ ወሞገሶ ሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ ሃሌ ፡ ሉያ ፣ ለተስፋሆሙ ፡፣ ለቅዱሳን ፡ አለ ፡ በዝየ.፡ ጻመዉ ፡ ይትፌ
son of the king of Kefaz, of a noble family, who reigned under the dependence of the king of Rome. I will also tell you that there was a poor man, son of the king of Rome, who went out suddenly from his house, without taking anything, except the staff only. I know this man very well; he wandered from desert to desert and from mountain to mountain: I have not learned of his death and I do not know his life, I do not know if he is alive or dead. There was also a poor man, son of a king, who kept vigil day and night, clothed in a sack, eating and drinking only on Sundays; he lived thus and died glorious. The angels of light took his soul and gave him a place in the kingdom of heaven. And now listen, O my brother, that I may speak to you; do not believe the punishment of God, your Creator, to be too hard, for He chastises those whom He loves." This monk said to Abba Kiros: "That is just and that is fitting; I receive what I deserve because of my sins." Having said these words, he fell silent. On this day, Abba Kiros prayed to God to make this sick man die; immediately came Our Lord Jesus Christ, to Him glory! with thousands and thousands of angels who glorified Him, sang, and said: "Alleluia to our God! Alleluia to the king and to the splendor of the just! Alleluia to the hope of the saints, who have endured

ሥሔሑ ፡፡ ወርእዮ ፣ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ ሰገደ ፡ ዲበ ፣ ምድር ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ወአ ምላኪየ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ አብጽሐከ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ መጻአኩ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ አስ መ ፡ ጸዋዕከኒ ፡፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፣ ኪሮስ ፡ ኅድጎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ነዳይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርፍ ፡ እ ስመ ፡ፅዕረ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡=፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እንከ ፡ ንሣእ ፡ ወወሀበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ጽጌ፣ አፒልያኖስ ፡፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ውግር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ነዳይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአዩ ፡ ሠ ራዊተ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አፈቅረከ ፣ ፈድፋደ = ወገብረ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፣ በከመ፣0፣ አዘዞ ፡ እግዚአነ ፡ ወወፅአት ፣ ነፍሱ ፣ ፍጡነ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፃዕር ፡ ወሕማም ፡ ወተመጠ ዋ ፡ አግዚእነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ወሰዓማ ፡ ወአንበራ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ሠረገላ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወአ ፅረጋ ፡ እንተ ፡ መንገለ ፡ አውኪያኖስ ፡ ወሜማ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት =፡ ወበ ከየ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተዓፅብ ። ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ዘከመ፣ አዕረፈ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ሚላዕሌየ ፡ ወነጸሮ ፣ አባ ፣ ኪሮስ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኢኮነ፣ ሰብአ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አላ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅብር ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወአምዕ ፀብ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ጻቅዱሳነ ፡ ወአምጽኡ ፣ ማዕጠንተ ፡ ወአምሐሎሙ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮ ስ ፣ በስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኢታምጽኡ ፡ ማዕጠንተክሙ ፡ ርኩስተ፣ ወኅድግዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካና ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ እለ ፡ የዓጥንዎ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ቅብርዎ ፡ ወተበሀሉ፣፣
labors here below, they will rejoice." At this sight, Abba Kiros prostrated himself on the ground and said: "O my Lord and God, what cause has brought You here?" Our Lord said to him: "I have come to you, because you called Me." Abba Kiros said to him: "Give the rest of death to this poor man, for he has suffered much." He said to him: "Well, take!" He put in his hand a flower of "apilyanos" and said to him: "Throw it on the face of this poor man, so that the armies of the angels may see how much I love you." Abba Kiros did as Our Lord had ordered him; immediately, his soul, of Misa'el, went out without pain and without sorrow; Our Lord received it in His hands, kissed it, and put it on His chariot beside Him; He made it ascend to the side of the ocean and gave it a place in the kingdom of heaven. Abba Kiros wept, astonished; he went to the superior and told him how Misa'el had died; the superior answered him: "What does it matter to me!" Abba Kiros fixed his gaze on him and said: "This is not a man, but a demon." He begged him to bury the body of Misa'el, and it is with difficulty that he gave the order to seven saints, monks, to bury him. They brought a censer; Abba Kiros adjured them in the name of God, saying: "Do not bring your impure censer, leave it in its place; those who will burn the incense are ready in this place, but bury him instead." These saints, monks, said to one another: "Where is this monk from?" They returned into the house, murmuring

against him. The four archangels rose and incensed his body with myrrh, stacte, "luzan", sandarac, cumin, "tefah", and "me'er"; the house was filled with the smoke of the incense. The monks said to one another: "Does he know sorcery? Where does all that come from to him?" It is that they had not perceived the angels. Then, they took his body and buried it in the convent, in the sepulcher of the strangers; a spring of clear water gushed from it, which healed all the sick and the infirm. May his blessing be with us, forever and ever! Amen.]
ዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለሚሳኤል ፡ ሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፣ አምተፅዕሮ ፡፡ እደ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ እንተ ፡ ቀበሮ ፡፡ ለአለ ፡ መነንዎ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ወኢሐወፁ ፡ ማኅደሮ ፡=፡ ያቅልል ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ለጌጋይክሙ ፡ ጾሮ ፡=፡
[Salutations to Misa'el (P: Mika'el)! When he rested from his afflictions, he who was buried by the hand of Kiros, he said to those who had despised him and had not visited his dwelling: "May God lighten the weight of your fault and forgive it, for, thanks to your iniquitous oppression, I have entered the dwelling of Christ."]
እስመ ፡ በግፍዕክሙ ፡ ቦእኩ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ማኅደሮ ።፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቢማ ፣ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ ብህንሳ ፡ አምላዕ ሳይ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ባዕለ ፣ ሠናየ ፡ ወመፍቀሬ ፡ ነዳያን ፡፡ ወ ኮነ ፣ ስዩመ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ሀገር ፡ ወርእዮ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በራ
On this day also is Saint Abba Bima of the city of Behnesa, in Upper Egypt. This saint was a man rich in goods and a lover of the poor; he was governor of this city; he saw, in a vision, Our Lord Jesus Christ, who was much brighter than the light of the sun. Jesus greeted him and said to him: "Arise, go to the prefect and confess my name, for behold

I have prepared for you a crown of joy." When he was awakened from his sleep, he arose, gave all his goods to the poor and the unfortunate; then he prayed, left the city, went to his city of Behnesa, and confessed Our Lord Jesus Christ before the prefect Luqyanos; when the latter learned that he was the governor of the city of Tankalawos (Bankalawos), he asked him for the sacred vessels of the Christian churches of his city and said to him: "Sacrifice to the gods." The saint replied, saying: "Of the sacred vessels of the Church, there does not remain the slightest thing. As for worshipping impure idols, I will not lend an ear to your words and your order, and I will not sacrifice to them, because I honor Our Lord Jesus Christ, Son of the Living God, Creator of heaven and earth and of all that is therein." The prefect was irritated and gave orders to cut off the saint's tongue with a sword; God restored to him an intact tongue without lesion, as it was before. He was tortured in a wine press and fixed with nails to an iron bed; fire was lit under him. God delivered him from his torture; He brought him out safe and sound, without a wound. Then, the prefect sent him to the city of Alexandria. Our Lord Jesus Christ appeared to him and strengthened him. Then, he was put in prison; he performed great wonders there and

ኡእ.ት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ውስቴ”ቱ ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ዐበይተ ፡ ወሀለወት ፡ አኅቱ ፡ ለዮልያኖስ ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፣ አቅፋስ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ተጽዕነ ፡ ዲቤሃ ፣ ጋኔን ፣ ወአውዕኦ ፡ ቅዱ ስ ፡ ለጋኔን ፣ እምላዕሌሃ ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ኩላ ፡ ሀገር ፣ ወብዙኃን ፡ አ ምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ በእዴሁ ፡ ወኮኑ ፣ ሰማዕተ ። ወተምዐ ፡፣ መኩንን ፡ወ «. ኤነኖ ፣ በዘዚአሁ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ በመ”ንኩራክራት ፣ ወመልሑ ፡፣ ጽፍራተ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወ እገሪሁ ፡ ወሰሐብዎ ፡፣ እንዘ ፣ እሱር ፡ በሰናስለ ፣ ሐጺን ፣ ”ወገደፍዎ ፤ ውስተ ፣ ምንዳ ደ ፡ ብለኔ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሰቀሉ ፣ እብነ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዱ ፡ ወወገርዎ ፡ ው ስተ ፡ ባሕር ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ያድኅኖ ፡፣ እምዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወያነሥኦ ፡ ጥዑ የ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። እምዝ ፣ ወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወድኅነ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወቆመ፣ ውስቴታ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይጹሊ ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ መኩንን ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ላፅላ ይ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወኩነንዎ ፣ በህየ ፣ ወሰቀልዎ ፣ ቀጐልዮሊተ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ወደይዎ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ጤገን ፡ ወአንደዱ ፣ መትሕቴሁ ፡ ወእግዚእነ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወያድኅኖ ፡ ወተካየይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ተዝካሮ ፡ አው ፡ ዘይጽሕፍ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ አው ፡ ዘያነብቦ ፡ ይጽሕፍ ፡ስሞ ፣ ውስተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ሕይወት ዞ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ መ
great miracles. There was the sister of Yulyanos of the city of Agfas who was possessed by an impure spirit; the Saint cast this impure spirit out of her. His fame spread throughout the city; many, through the intermediary of this saint, believed in Our Lord Jesus Christ and became martyrs. The judge was irritated and condemned him to all sorts of tortures, to the wheel; they tore out the nails of his hands and feet, they dragged him, tied with iron chains, and threw him into the furnace of a bath. Then, they hung a large stone around his neck and threw him into the sea; God delivered him from all these tortures and raised him up safe and sound, without a wound. Then, they put him in the fire; he came out of it safe and sound: in the fire, he stood in prayer. When the prefect was weary of it, he sent him to Upper Egypt; there, he was tortured and crucified head down; then he was thrown into a pan and fire was lit underneath; Our Lord appeared to him, delivered him, and made him the promise that whoever would make his commemoration, write the book of his combats, or read it, would have his name written in the book of life. When the prefect was weary of torturing him, he ordered his head to be cut off by the sword;


[p. 289] {#po.7.p289}
ኩንን ፣ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፣ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወነሥኡ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ አባ ፡ ቢማ ፣ አግብርቲሁ ፡ ለዮ ልያኖስ ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ አቅፋሐስ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡፣ ኅበ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፣ ውስተ ፣መ ካን ፡ ሠናይ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ ስደት ። ወሐነጹ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲ ያን ፣ ሠናይተ ፡ ወደብረ ፡ ወአንበሩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ተኣምራ ት ፡ ወመንክራት ፣፡ ብዙኃት ። ወሥጋሁ ፣ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ይፌውሶሙ ፡ ለዙሎሙ ፣ ድ ውያን ፤ እለ ፡ ይበጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወይትአምኅዎዖ ፡ በአሚን ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይ
he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. The servants of Yulyanos of the city of Agfahas took the body of Saint Abba Bima, carried it near his city, and deposited it in a beautiful place, a sanctuary, until the days of the persecution were finished. A beautiful church and a convent were built for him, and his body was deposited there, which performed wonders and miracles in great number; his body, until now, heals all the sick who come to visit it and kiss it with faith. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer, and may his blessing be with us! So be it.
ምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፣ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፣ ለአባ ፣ ቢማ ፡ እንተ ፡ ነጸሮ ፡ በራእይ ፡፡ ለገጸ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ስቡሕ ፡ እምነ ፡ ፀሓይ ።፡፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ሐሚሞ ፡ በዐውደ ፡ ስቃይ ፡፡ በዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ክሂል ፡ ወበዚአሁ ፡ ፅበይ ፡፡
[Salutations to Abba Bima, who beheld, in a vision, the face of Christ brighter than the sun; when because of Him he had suffered all the tortures at the tribunal of torments, his cut-off tongue returned to its natural state, with all its strength and greatness.]
ኀበ ፣ ህላዌሁ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ልሳኑ ፡ ውቁይ ፡1 ወበዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡፣ ክፅበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ኪሮስ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ የኃድር ፤ በገዳመ ፡ ምዕራብ ፡ በቅሩብ ፣ እምሲኦል ።-ወውእቱ ፡ እጉሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ቴዎዶስዮ ስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ዓመፃ ፡ አለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኃደገ ፡ ኩ ሎ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ እምሀገሩ ፡ ወመርሖ ፡ እግዚአነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኀ
On this day also, died the holy Father Abba Kiros (Cyrus), who lived in the western desert, near hell. He was the brother of the Emperor Tewodosyos (Theodosius) the Great; when this saint saw the iniquity of those who are in the world, he abandoned all his goods and left his city. Our Lord

Jesus Christ guided him and made him arrive at this western desert; he remained there alone for many years; during all this time, he saw no man, nor the beasts of the desert. There was in the desert of Scetis a priest by the name of Abba Bamu (Pambo); he is the one who had buried Saint Ilarya, daughter of the Emperor Zeno. This priest said: While I was alone in my church, I heard a voice, coming from heaven, which said to me: "Abba Bamu, arise, go and enter into the middle of the desert, to bury the body of a holy hermit, who has fulfilled my will very well, better than many holy monks." Immediately, I arose, all joyful, I walked in the desert for three days, I arrived at a cave, a cell, and I knocked at the door; an old hermit opened for me; we greeted one another. I questioned him, saying to him: "My Father, is there another hermit in this desert?" He answered me that there was one. - "How long," I said to him, "have you been dwelling in this desert? What is your name?" He said to me: "My name is Sem'on (Simeon); it has now been seventy years that I have been dwelling in this desert." I left him and I walked for three other days; I met another solitary; his name was Amon; I greeted him and said to him: "Is there another hermit

in this desert?" He answered me that there was one; I greeted him, I walked four (P: 40) other days and I arrived at the cave of this Abba Cyrus; I knocked at the door of his cave, saying: "Bless me, O holy Abba." At that hour, he said to me: "Your coming to me today is good, be welcome, O Abba Bamu, saint of God; enter in peace." I entered to him, I greeted him, and I received his blessing. I saw the grace of God on his face: the hair of his head and his beard were white as hail, as snow. When I had sat down and he had pressed me to his bosom, he kissed me and said to me: "It has been fifty-seven years that I have inhabited this cave, in expectation of this hour." Immediately, he fell ill and lay down, a prey to troubles, during the whole night; in the morning, a great light shone in his cave; a man all shining with light entered; in his hands was a luminous cross; he remained near Saint Cyrus, kissed him, blessed him, consoled him, gave him peace, and disappeared. I, I was in terror; I said to the Saint: "Who is he who is clothed with such great glory and who shines in this manner?" He said to me: "My son, it is Our Lord Jesus Christ, Son of the living God; he has the habit of coming to me every day and consoling me." At nine o'clock on

Saturday, I heard a great clamor that reached up to heaven; the mountains and the stars were shaken by their cries. I said: "My Father, what are this clamor and these voices that I hear?" He answered me: "My son, these are the cries of the sinners who are in hell. Our Lord causes their tortures to cease because of his holy Resurrection, from nine o'clock on Saturday until the setting of the sun on Sunday; that is why they praise God for making them rest on Sunday." As for me, I was in great astonishment and I praised God. Then, it was the 7th of Hamle, Saint Abba Cyrus began to weep, saying: "A great pillar has fallen today in Upper Egypt." It was Saint Abba Sinoda, chief of the hermits. During the night from Saturday to Sunday, Saint Abba Cyrus was agitated by his grave illness; behold, a great light shone in the cave; Our Lord Jesus Christ entered; he stood at the head of Saint Abba Cyrus; the latter said to the Savior: "My Lord and my God, bless this man, for he has come from a far country." Our Savior said to me: "Abba Bamu, my chosen one, have confidence and may my peace and my blessing be with you; now write the combats of this saint, what you have heard said, what you have seen by yourself, and what you will hear


[p. 293] {#po.7.p293}
ኅሩይየ ፡ ጽናዕ ፣ ወሰላምየ ፤ ወበረከትየ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌክ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ጸሐፍ ፤ ገድ ሎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘሰማዕከ ፣ ወዘ”ርኢከ ፡ ወዘትሰምዕ ፡ ክፅበ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መድኃኒ ነ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፣ ኦኅሩይየ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ ኢትሕዘን ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ት መውት ፡፣ እስመ ፡ ሞትከሰ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ሞት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አላ ፡ ሕይወት ፣ ዘለዓለም ። ወኩ ሉ ፡ ዘይጽሕፍ ፡ ገድለከ ፡ አው ፡ ዘያነብቦ ፣፡ አው ፡ ዘይሰምዕ ፡ አንብቦቶ ፣ ወይገብር ፤ ተዝካረከ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ በፍቅር ፡ አነ ፡ እሬስዮ ፡ ይርፍቅ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ከ ብካብ ፣ ዘ፲፻ዓመት ፡ በደብረ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ወእኃልቆ ፡ ምስለ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወኩ ሉ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይሁብ ፡ ሞርባነ ፡ አው ፣ ምጽዋተ ፡ አው ፡ ያኃቱ ፡ ማኅቶተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስምከ ፡ አነ ፡ አዓስዮ ፤ በመንግሥትየ ፣ መክዕቢተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ። አው ፣ ዘይጽሕፍ ፣ መጽሐፈ ፡ ገድልከ ፡ አነ ፡ አሰጥጥ ፣ መጽሐፈ ፣ ኃጣውኢሁ ፡ ወእጽሕፎ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ሕይወት ጦ። ወዘይገብር ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ምስኪናን ፣ በእለተ ፣ ተዝ ካርከ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ አመ ፡ ቿለወርኃ ፣ ሐምሌ ፡ አነ ፡ እሬስዮ ፣ ይርአይ ፣ ዘኢያስተር ኢ ፡ ለዓይን ፣ ወዘኢይሰማፅ ፡፤ ለአዝን ፣ ወዘኢይትሔለይ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወ አቄርቦሙ ፤ ምሥጢራተ ፡፣ ቅድሳት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘበር ፡ ወእባር ኮሙ ፡ ወአባርክ ፡ ቤቶሙ ፤ ወአልህቅ ፣ ውሉዶሙ ፣ ወኢይኃጥኡ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፤
again." Our Savior said to the saint Abba: "O Abba Cyrus, my chosen one, do not worry about the fact that you are going to die, for your death will not be a death, but the life of eternity. Every man who will write your combats, or who will read them, or who will hear them read, or who will celebrate your memory, with love, on earth, I will make him sit with me at the wedding, at the messianic banquet on Mount Zion, for a thousand years, and I will count him in the number of my saints. Whoever will give an offering, or will give alms, or will cause a torch to burn because of your name, I will reward him in my kingdom, by giving him back much more. He who will write the book of your combats will have the book of his sins torn up, and I will write his name in the book of life. To all those who will show mercy to the poor, on the day of your commemoration, that is to say the 8th of the month of Hamle, I will make them see what the eye does not see, what the ear does not hear, what the heart of man does not conceive; I will make them approach the holy Mystery, in the first-born church; I will bless them and bless their houses; I will make their children grow; they will lack none of the goods of this world. Now, my beloved Abba Cyrus, I will make you rest from the fatigues


[p. 294] {#po.7.p294}
እምሠናያተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ዓለም ። ወይእዜኒ ፣ ኦፍቁርየ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ አነ ፣ አዓርፈከ ፣ እምጻማ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፣ ወአፈልሰከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካናት ፡ ብርሃናዊት ፡ ውስተ ፣ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወእንዘ ፤ መድኃኒነ ፡ ይብል ዘንተ ፣ ወናሁ ፣ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ መጽእአ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ መሰንቆሁ ፣ ወሰንቀወ ፡ ባቲ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፡ ዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ እን ተ ፡ ገብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ንትፈሣሕ ፣ ወንትሐሠይ ፡ ባቲ ። ወይቤሎ ፣ መድኃኒነ፣ ለአባ › ኪሮስ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ዳዊ"ት ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ንግር ፡ ዘትፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዘምር ፡ ለከ። ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ትፈቅድኑ ፡ እዜምር ፡ ለከ ፡ በአይ ፡ አውታር ፡ ወበአይ ፡ ዜማ ፡ ወበአ ይ ፡ ጾታ ፡ በቀዳማይኑ ፡ አው ፣ በዳግማይኑ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፲አውታር ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ አነ ፡ እፈቱ ፡ እስማዕ ፡ ፤አውታር ፣ ወዜማሆሙ ፣ ወጾታሆሙ ። ወአስተ ዳለወ ፣ ዳዊት ፡ መሰንቆሁ ፡ ወሰንቀወ ፡ ባቲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ለጻድቅ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። እግዚኦ ፡ አነ ፡ ገብርከ ። ገብርከ ፡ ወልደ ፣ ዓመትከ ። ወክ ዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወርዘውኩሂ ፡ ወረሣእኩ ። ወጻድቅሰ ፣ ዘይትገደፍ ፡ ኢርኢኩ ። ወጸርኃ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወአሠነየ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ወሆከ ፡ መሰንቆሁ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ ነበረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕፅኑ ፣ ለመድኃኒነ ፡ ወነሥአ ፣ መድኃኒነ ፣ ወ” ሰዓማ ፡ ወወሀባ ፡ ለ ሚካኤል ። ወአነ ፡ በሙ ፡ ቀበርኩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ወፃእነ ፡ እምበዐት ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ደብር ፡ ምሉእ ፡ እምኃይላት ፡ ብርሃናዊያት ፡ ወሊቃናተ ፡
of this world and will make you pass into the luminous places, into the joys of eternity." While Our Lord was speaking to him in these terms, behold, the prophet David arrived, with his cithara on which he sang, saying: "In this day that God has made, let us rejoice and be in gladness." Our Savior said to Abba Cyrus: "Cyrus, behold David has come; say what you want him to sing to you." David said to him: "With what strings do you want me to sing? With what song, with what rhythm and what mode! With the first or with the second, and so on until that of ten strings." Saint Abba Cyrus replied: "I desire to hear the ten-stringed instrument, its sounds and its mode." David tuned his instrument and played it, saying: "Glorious is the death of the just before God; O my God, I am your servant, your servant, the son of your handmaid." He said again: "I have been young and I have grown old, without ever seeing a just man who was abandoned." David cried out with a beautiful voice and made his instrument play; behold, the soul of Saint Abba Cyrus remained in the bosom of our Savior; our Savior took it, kissed it, and gave it to Michael. As for me, Bamu, I buried the body of Saint Abba Cyrus; when we came out of the cave, I saw the whole mountain filled with luminous armies and


[p. 295] {#po.7.p295}
ሰራዊተ ፡ መላእክት ። ወአንበረ ፣ መድኃኒነ ፡ መስቀሎ ፡ ወሐተማ ፡ ለበዓት ፣ ዘውስቴ ታ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ወወሀበኒ ፣ መድኃኒነ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ወዐርገ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሰማያት ፣ በ ዐቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወመላእክት ፡ ወሊቃነ ፣ መላእክት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሴብሑ ፣ ቅድሜሁ ፣ ወዳዊትኒ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዜምር ፡ በመሰንቆሁ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ኪሮስ ፣ ወመ”ድኃኒነ ፣ በማእከሌሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ፡ በነፍሱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡፥ ወዓርገ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ውስተ ፤ መንግሥቱ ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወአንሰ ፣ ባሙ ፣ ተረፍኩ ፣ ባሕቲ ትየ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ መካን ፣ ረከብኩ ፡ ርእስየ ፡ ኅበ ፡ በዐቱ ፡ ለአባ ፣፡ አሞን። ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ አንዕሰውኩ ፣ ፻መዓልተ ፡ ወበጻሕኩ ፣ ኀበ በዐቱ ፣ ለአባ፣ ስምዖ ን ። ወዓዲ ፡ አንሶሰውኩ ፣ ይመዓልተ ፣ ክል”እተ ፡ እስከ ፡ በጻሕኩ ፣ ኅበ ፡ ደብርየ፣በገ" ዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወተአማኅክዎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ መነኮሳት ። ወነገርክዎሙ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ ለ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ኪሮስ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ርእየ ፣ ወዘከመ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሲኖዳ ። ወሶበ ፣ ሰምዑ ፣ መነኮሳት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘይሁብ ፣ ዛተ ፣ ጸጋ ፣ ለአለ ፣ ይፈርህዎ ። ወጸሐፍኩ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ወፈነውኩ ፡ ኀበ ፣ ዙሉ ፡ አብያተ ፣ ክርስቲያ
chiefs of the angelic armies; our Savior deposited his cross and sealed the cave, in which was the body of the Saint; our Savior gave me peace (said to me: "Peace be upon you") and went back up to heaven, in great pomp, while, before him, the angels and archangels glorified him. David played his instrument before the soul of Abba Cyrus; our Savior was in the midst of them, rejoicing in the soul of Saint Abba Cyrus; he went up with it (made it go up) into his eternal kingdom. As for me, Bamu, I remained alone in that place; I found myself near the cave of Abba Amon; after having walked three days, I arrived at the cave of Abba Sem'on; again, I walked for three other days, until the moment when I arrived at my convent in the desert of Scetis. I greeted all the monks and told them the combats of Saint Abba Cyrus; I told them how he had seen and heard and how he had prophesied concerning the death of Abba Sinoda. When the monks heard this account, they were in great astonishment and they praised God, who gives this grace to those who fear him. I wrote the combat, the life, of this saint, I sent it to all the churches and to all the monasteries; whoever

read the combats, the life, of this holy Father Abba Cyrus, found therein a subject of hope and consolation. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing and his intercession be with us, in the centuries of centuries! So be it. [Salutations to Abba Cyrus, who, with his lips, kissed the flame, and on whose back God was carried. So that his soul might depart without pain, David sang, on the instrument of the sweet psalm, saying: "Glorious is the death of the just."]

On this day, it is the feast of the holy Apostle Sem'on Qalayopa (Simeon Cleophas), martyr; this saint was named bishop of the city of Jerusalem, after Yagob (James), the brother of Our Lord. He converted a great number of Jews to the faith of Our Lord Jesus Christ. He performed many wonders and miracles and healings of the sick. The Emperor Aryanos had heard it said of him that he separated women from their husbands and that he ordered them to be chaste. He had him brought to him, and inflicted great tortures upon him; at that time, the saint was 120 years old. When he was weary of torturing him, he cut off his head with a sword; with him was a virgin, who followed him, named Tewona (Theone). May, through his prayer, God have mercy on us and may his blessing be with us. Amen.

On this same day, died the holy Father Kaladyanos (Claudian), patriarch of the city of Alexandria. He was the ninth in the series of patriarchs. This saint remained on the seat of Mark the Evangelist for fourteen years, guarding his flock, and died in peace. [It is also the commemoration of the ascetic Tewodros (Theodore), bishop of Qorontos (Corinth). May God have mercy on us through his prayer. Amen.]

he died in peace. May God have mercy on us, through his prayer, in the centuries of centuries. So be it.] READING FOR THE 10TH OF HAMLE (July 17). In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. Reading for the 10th of Hamle. On this day is the commemoration of Saint Tewodros (Theodore), martyr, bishop of the Pentapolis. When the impious Diocletian rendered divine honors to idols, he sent a prefect into each province, with orders to torture the Christians and to make them perish by all sorts of pains. He sent another prefect, named Felatos (Pilate), into the country of Afraqya (Africa) and the whole district. When he had arrived, he saw the whole country; they accused this Saint to him, as teaching the Christians; it had been a year since Abba Tewonas (Theonas) had ordained this Saint. The prefect had him brought and ordered him to sacrifice to the idols; the Saint answered him and said: "It is not fitting that we abandon the God creator of heaven and earth; we do not worship idols." The prefect took the word and said to him: "Is it that beside Artemis, Apollo, Artemida (Ἀρτέμιδα) and all


[p. 300] {#po.7.p300}
ርጥሚስ ፡ ወለአብሎን ፣ ወለአርጣሚዳ ፡ ወለኩሎሙ ፡ አማልክት ፡ ካልእኑ ፣ አምላክ ፣ ወኢኮኑኑ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ አማልክተ ። “ወአውሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እወ ፡ ኢኮኑ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ አላ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈጣሪሆሙ ። ወተምዓ ፣፡ መኩንን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጽንዓ ፣ ተሰጥዎቱ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንንዎ ። ወነበ ሩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቤንንዎ ፡ ጓግመዓልተ ፡ በቅሥፈት ፡ ወበስቅለት ፡ ወኩነኔ ፣ በመንኩራኩራ ት ፦ ወበውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ። ወኢፈርሀ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ ወኢሰምዓ ፡ ትእዛዞ ። ወሶ ቤሃ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፣ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ሰ
the gods, there are other gods? Are these not gods?" The Saint answered and said: "No, certainly, they are not gods, but it is Our Lord Jesus Christ who created them." The prefect was irritated by the firmness of his answer; he gave the order to torture him. For forty days, he underwent tortures, flagellation, crucifixion, the punishment of the wheel, imprisonment. These pains did not frighten him at all and he did not obey his orders, the prefect's. Immediately, on the order of the latter, they cut off his head with the sword. He received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us through his prayer. Amen.
ማያት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ ለታኦድሮስ ፡ ዘቀሠፍዎ ፡ አባለ ። ጓመዋፅለ ። በዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ድኅረ ፡ ተጋደለ ። ፡ ዓስበ ፣ ሐመ ፡ በመዋቅሕት ፡ ወዕሴተ ፣ በሰይፍ ፡ ተቀትለ ።
[Salutations to Ta'odros, whose limbs were tortured for forty days; after he had struggled in the midst of all these torments, in reward for what he had suffered in chains and in return for having been killed by the sword, he obtained the kingdom of heaven and received the crown of martyrdom.]
መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ አድም ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ቆሮን ቶስ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ አንስት ፣ ወ፪መኳንንት ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለው ፡ ይኬንንዎ ፣ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ሉ
On this day, there is also Tewodros (Theodore), martyr, bishop of the city of Qorontos (Corinth) and with him (O, A, P: three) women and two magistrates,


[p. 301] {#po.7.p301}
ኪዮስ ፣ ወድግናንዮስ ፣ ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፣ አስተዋደይዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እሉ ፡ ፪መኳንንት ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ ወውሕቱ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆዕስ፣፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ቆሮንቶስ ። ወአብጽሕዎ ፣ ወተስእልቻ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ሃይማኖቱ ፡ ወተአምነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ። ወኩነንዎ ፡ ወቀሠፍዎ ፡ በአብትር ፡ ወድሕከ ፡ ዲበ፣ ምድር ፡ ወረገፃ ፡ ለመንበረ ፣ ጣዖታት ፡ ወገፍትኦሙ ። ወተምውዑ ፡ አሉ ፡ ፪መኳንን ት ፡ ወኩነንቓ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ወፈሐቁ ፡ ቀሩስላቲሁ ፡ በአጽርቅተ ፡ ጸኾር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ጥ ሙቃን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሒአ ፡ ወፄው ። ወውእቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይረግሞሙ ፡ ለጣዖታት፡ወ ለመኳንንት ። ወካዕበ ፣ አዘዙ ፣፡ መኳንንት ፡ ወመተሩ ፣ ልሳኖ ። ወሶበ ፣ መተርዎ፡ነ ሥአቶ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ እምእሎን ፣ አንስት ፣ መሃይምናን ፡ እለ ፡ ነበራ ፡ በሀየ ። ወሶበ ፣ ሞ ቅሕዎ ፣ ለቅዱስ ፣ ነሥአ ፡ ልሳኖ ፡ እምይእቲ ፣ ብእሲት ፣ ወአንበሮ ፡ ዲበ ፣ ከርሠ፣ ወሰፍሐ ፡ ልሳኖ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፣ አፉሁ ። ወመጽአት ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ርግብ ፡ ጸዓዳ ፡ ወሰ ረረት ፡ አውዶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሰረረ ፡ ጣዎስ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መስኮት ። ወር እይዎሙ ፡ እሉ ፡ ፪መኳንንት ፡ ወአንከሩ ። "”ወሉኪዮስሰ ፡ አምነ ፡ በእግዚእነ › ኢየ ሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ”ወተምዓ ፡ ድግናንዮስ ፡ ወቀተሎን ፡ ለ፻የአንስት ፡ እምአለ ፡
who were to torture him, Lukyos and Degnanyos. Here is in what circumstances: when this Saint was accused before these two magistrates as being a Christian and bishop of the city of Corinth, they had him brought and questioned him concerning his faith; he confessed that he was a Christian; they punished him and beat him with rods. He dragged himself on the ground, struck the throne of the idols with his foot, and overturned them. The two magistrates were irritated and inflicted great tortures upon him. They scraped his wounds with pieces of cloth covered with hair, after having soaked them in vinegar and salt. He himself cursed the idols and the magistrates. On the order of the latter, they cut off his tongue. When they had cut off his tongue, one of the faithful women who were in that place took it. When he was put in prison, the Saint took back his tongue from this woman. He placed it on his bosom; he stretched out the tongue which entered his mouth. A white dove came to fly around the Saint. Then a peacock flew; it remained on the window; the two magistrates saw them and were in astonishment. Lukyos believed immediately in Our Lord Jesus Christ; but Degnanyos became angry and killed three of the women who followed the Saint. When the Saint rendered his soul, the dove and the peacock flew and departed


[p. 302] {#po.7.p302}
ያንሶስዋ ፣ ድኅሬሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ። ወሶበ ፡ መጠወ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ሰረሩ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ርግ ብ ፡ ወጣዎስ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወአንከረ ፡ ድግናንዮስ ፡ እምዝንቱ ። ወአጠየቆ ፡ ሉኪ ዮስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወአምነ፣ ድግናንዮስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክዕበ ፡ በአግዚእነ ፣ ክርስቶስ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፣ ውስተ ባሕር ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ቆሮንቶስ ፡ እስከ ፣ ሀገረ ፣ ቆጵሮስ ። ወረከቡ ፣ ካልአ ፣ መኩንነ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይቤንኖሙ ፣ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ሉኪዮስ ፣ በቅድሜሁ ፡ በኅቡእ ፣፡ እምድግ ናንዮስ ፡ ወገፍትአ ፡ መንበረ ፤ ጣዖታት ፣ ወተአምነ ፣ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወመተ ሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፣ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፣ አክሊለ ፣ ስምዕ ። ወጾሮ ፣ ድግናንዮስ ፡ ወገነዞ ፡ ወቀበ ሮ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ቆመ ፣ ድግናንዮስ ፣ በቅድመ ፡፣ ውእቱ ፣ መኩንን ፣ ወተአምነ፣ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወመተረ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፣ አክሊ”ለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመን
immediately. This was for Degnanyos a subject of astonishment. Lukyos made it known to him and said to him that the faith of Our Lord Jesus Christ is the truth. Degnanyos, he too, believed in Our Lord Jesus Christ. They went then on the sea, from the city of Corinth to the country of Qopros (Cyprus); they met then another magistrate who punished the Christians. Lukyos stood before him, in secret from Degnanyos; he overturned the throne of the idols and confessed his faith in Our Lord Jesus Christ. They cut off his head with the sword, and he received the crown of martyrdom. Degnanyos took him, wrapped him in a shroud, and buried him. Then he stood before the magistrate, to confess his faith in Our Lord Jesus Christ. The magistrate cut off his head with the sword, and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us all, through their prayer. Amen.
ግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለዙሎሙ ፡ አሜን ። ሰላም ፡ እብል ፣ ለቴዎድሮስ ፡ ፅዑር ፡፡ ዘዘበጥዎ ፡ በበትር ፡፡ ወፈሐቁ ፡ ፉሞስሎ ፡ በአጽርቅተ ፡ ፀጉር ፡፡ ሰላም ፡ለክልኤ ፣ መኳንንተ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ወም"”ክር ፡=፡ ምስለ ፡ አንስት ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ማኅበር ፡፡
[I say salutations to Theodore, overwhelmed with pains. They beat him with rods; they scraped his wounds with pieces of cloth covered with hair. Salutations to the two magistrates full of wisdom and counsel, with the women, his company, of Theodore.]


[p. 303] {#po.7.p303}
ወበዛቲ ፡፣ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ አፅረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ መስተጋድል ፡ አባ ፡ ኅልያን ፡ ለዝን ቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ይስጣስ ፡ ወስመ ፡ እሙ ፡ ክልምና ፡ ወደወሉ ፡ ዓይነ፣ ፀሓይ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አመ ፡ ውርዛዌሁ ፡ ነሀቤ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወብሩር ፡ ወውዱስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በግብ ረ ፡ እደዊሁ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ መጽአት ፣ ኀቤሁ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ዓረብ፣ ወሰአለቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንህብ ፡ ላቲ ፡ አዕኑገ ፡፣ ለእዘኒሃ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ኀሠሠ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ዓስበ ፡ ወአድነነት ፡ ርእሳ ፡ በቅድሜሁ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ እመሰ ፡ ትፈቅድ ፡ እም ኔየ ፡ ዘይፈቅድዎ ፡ ዕደው ፡ እምአንስት ፡ ነየ ፡ አነ ፡ ቅድሜከ ፡ ወካልእሰ ፡ ንዋይ ፡ አል ብየ ፡ ወሰሚዖ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ይቤላ ፡ አንቲሰ ፡ ወለቱ ፡ ለመኩንነ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ ሖሪ ፡ እም ኔየ ፡ ወኢታርአእይኒ ፡ ገጸኪ ።፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ጐንደየ ፡ ሕቀ ፣ ውስተ ፣፡ ማኅደሩ ፡እንዘ ፣ ያዜክራ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡፤ ሞተ ፡ ወኩነኔ ፣ ወተንሥአ ፣ ዘረወ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ነዳያን ፡ ወ ምስኪናን ፡ ወተሰነአለ ፡ ለወላዲቱ ፡ ወአትረፈ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ክፍላ ፣ እምንዋዩ ፡ ወጾረ ፡ ስንቀ ፡ ዘየአክሎ ፡ ለየዕለት ፡ ወሖረ ፤ ውስተ ፣ ገዳም ።፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከሀሊ ፡ ሶ በ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ንጽሐ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፡ አቅረበ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ርኅቀተ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ኤርትራ፤ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ነዊሞ ፣ አስተርአይዖ ፡ ቦዕደው ፡ ለባስያነ ፡ ፀዓዳ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትመረጐዙ ፣ መስቀለ ፡ ዘያበርህ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሓይ ፡ ወአንቅህዎ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ም
On this day, the holy ascetic Abba Helyan also died. The father of this saint was named Yestas and his mother Kelmena; he was from the district of Ayna Sahay (Heliopolis). In his youth, he worked with gold and silver; he was highly praised for the work of his hands. One day, a woman from the land of the Arabs came to him and begged him to chisel some earrings for her. When his work was finished, he demanded his wages; she bowed before him and said to him: "If you desire from me what men ask of women, here I am, ready before you; but I have no other goods to give you." Having heard these words, he said to her: "You are the daughter of the prince of demons; depart from me and show me your face no more." Afterwards, he remained for some time in his house, reflecting within himself on death and the last judgment; he arose, distributed his goods to the poor and the unfortunate, and bade farewell to his mother, to whom he left his share of the goods; he took enough to nourish himself for three days and went into the desert. When God Almighty saw the purity of his thought and his spirit, He brought distances closer for him; in one day, Abba Helyan arrived at the Red Sea. While he was sleeping, three men dressed in white appeared to him; they were leaning on a cross as bright as the sun; they awakened him and took him with them; when he had arrived at the entrance of a


[p. 304] {#po.7.p304}
ስሌሆሙ ፡ ወዕበ፡፣ በጽሐ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ዓፀጆዶ ፡ ገነት ፡ ወወሀብዎ ፡ በትረ ፡ ወመስቀለ ፡፣ዘ እምፅንቀ፦ ፡፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ሀለው ፣ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ጸለዩ ፡፣ ኅቡረ ፣ ወበጊዜ ፡ ስግደቱ ፡ ሶ በ ፡አልዓለ ፡ ርእሶ ፣ ኢረከበ ፣ አሐደኒ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተፈልጦቶ ሙ ፡፡ ወሰባ ፡ ኖኃ ፡ ንብረቱ ፣ ለመደ ፡ ብሕትውና ፡፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴሰይ ፡ ሐመ ልማለ ፣ ገነት ፡ ወይሰቲ ፣ ማየ ። ወእመ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ውስተ0፣ በዓታት ፡ ወገዳማት ፣ ወመካናት ፡ ታበርሀ ፡፣ ሎቱ ፡ በትረ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወድቀትኒ ፡ ት ጠውም ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ልብሱ ፡ እምልሕጸ ፡ እንትኩ ፡ አዕዋም።
garden, they gave him a staff and a cross of precious stones; they prayed together in this garden; at the moment he had prostrated himself, he raised his head and found no one there anymore. He wept because of their departure, and as his stay was prolonged, he became accustomed to the life of an anchorite; he remained eating the greenery of the garden and drinking water. When, in the night, he wanted to go visit some cavern (hermit's cell), or convent, or holy place, the staff of the Cross would light his way and, at the same time, protect him from falls; his clothing was formed from the bark of the trees of the place.
ወሰይጣንሰ ፡ ርጉም ፡ ዕበ ፣ ርእየ ፣ ተጋድሎቶ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ እኩያን ፣ወ ነገሮሙ ፡-እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ሀሎ ፡ መዝገብ ፣ ኅቡእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወለእመ ፡ አኃዝ ክምዎ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ዘየአቅቦ ፡ ትረክቡ ፡ ወወሰዶሙ ፡ ወአብጽሖሙ ፡ ማዕ”ይተ ፡ ውእ ቱ ፡ ፈለግ ፣ ወበጺሖሙ ፡ ኃጥኡ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ኅበ ፡ የዓድዉ ፣ ማየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሬእዩ ፡ ተ ስእኖሙ ፡ በጺሐ ፤ህየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስተዩ ፡ ወጸምኡ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወክዕበ ፡ ተመሰለ ፡ ሰይ ጣን ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ አርዌ ፡ መፍርህ ፡፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኅልያን ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እፎ፣
When the cursed Satan saw how he practiced asceticism, he came to wicked men and said to them: "There is a treasure hidden in the desert; if you seize the man who guards it, you will find it." He guided them and led them to the edge of the river; but, when they had arrived, they could no longer find the place to cross the river; although they saw the water, they could not reach it to drink, even though they were very thirsty. Again, Satan took on the appearance of a frightening serpent; he approached Saint Helyan and said to him: "How is it that you have no compassion for your companions who are suffering from thirst and do not give them something to drink?" He thought


[p. 305] {#po.7.p305}
አንተ ፡ ዘኢትምህሀር ፡ አብያጺከ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይጸምኡ ፣ ዘኢታሰትዮሙ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወበእንተ ዝ ፡ መሰሎ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይአኅዝፆ ፡ ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘለፎ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡ በ አፈ ፡ አርዌ። ቀድሐ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወዓደወ ፡ ያስትዮሙ ። ወዕበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ንዴቶ ፣ ተራኅርጉጐ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወፈቀዱ ፡፣ የሀብዎ ፣ ልብሰ ፣፡ ወኢተመጠወ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ወተመ ይጠ ፤ ውስተ ፡ማኅደሩ ፡ ወለሰይጣንሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡ "ተጸርዓ ፣ ትምይንቱ ፡ ተመሰለ ፡ ክፅ በ "በአምሳለ ፡መነኮሳት ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስሕትዎ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ዓተበ ፡ ላዕሌ ሆሙ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ጐዩ ፡ ተሐፊሮሙ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፍ ልሰቱ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እሉ ፡ የፅደው ፡ ዘአስተርአይዎ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወጸሐፉ ፡ ሎቱ ፥፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ቀበርዎ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለ ም፡፣፡አሜን።ገ
that they would have taken him by this means. As for the Saint, he thought that God was rebuking him through the mouth of the serpent; he drew water for them and crossed the river to give them something to drink. When they saw his indigence, they had pity on him; they wanted to give him clothes, but he accepted nothing and returned to his dwelling. As for Satan, since his fraud had not succeeded, he again took the likeness of monks, who came to the saint in order to seduce him; but, when the latter had made the sign of the cross over them, they fled, covered with shame. Then, when the moment of his death approached, the three men who had appeared to him before came to him and wrote the account of his life; when he was dead, they buried him. May God, through his intercession, have mercy on us for ever and ever! Amen.
ሰላም ፡ እብል ፡ ለብእሲ ፣ ምእመን ፣ እንተ ፡ ይሰመይ ፡ ኅልያን፤ ከመ ፡ ግዕዘ ፡ ሠላስ ፡ ዕደው ፡ ኅብረ ፡ ፀዓዳ ፡ ልቡሳን ፤ ያበርህ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መጽልም ፡ መካን ፡፡
[I offer a greeting to the faithful man who was called Helyan; in the same way as the three men clothed in white garments, a light that came out of the cross, the staff that he had in his hand, lit his way in the dark places where he went.]
አምበትረ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ መስቀል ፡ መጺኦ ፡ ብርሃን ፡፡ ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ተዝክሮሙ- ፡ ለማር ፡ ማጥራ ፡ወእንድራዎስ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆ
[On this day also, memory of Mag Matra and of Endrawos, bishop of Qafala (O: Qafahala); dedication of the church of Sargis (Sergius) in Egypt (Old Cairo?);


[p. 306] {#po.7.p306}
ይስ ፡ ዘቀፈላ ፣ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ለሰርጊስ ፡ በግብጽ ፡ ወስምዓ ፡ እለ ፡ ኮ ኑ ምስለ አባ ፡ ኤሲ ፡ ዛወ፳ነፍስ ፡ ወ፪ሕራ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለ መ፣ ዓለም ፡ አሜን።
martyrdom of the companions of Abba Esi, to the number of fifty-five persons, and of four soldiers. May their blessing be with us for ever and ever! Amen.]

READING FOR THE 11TH OF HAMLE (July 18) In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day were the holy martyrs Yohannes and Sem'on (John and Simeon), his paternal cousin, from the city of Sermalas (P: Sarmalas). The mother of Saint John was barren; his father prayed to Our Lord Jesus Christ to give him a son; and he made a vow to consecrate him to the service of God all his life. Saint John the Baptist appeared to him in a vision and announced to him that God would give him a son; when he had this saint as a child, he called him Yohannes. John had previously built a church under the name of John the Baptist; when the young John had grown up and was eleven years old (P, O, A: 12), his father gave him sheep to guard: each day, he gave all his food to the shepherds and to those who passed on the road; and he fasted until the evening, every day. When his father learned of this, he came to him in the field to know the truth of what had been reported to him about him. The young John, fearing that his

father would beat him, wanted to flee; his father said to him: "Show me your food for today." He answered him by saying: "Enter to see." When his father had entered his dwelling, he saw the basket filled with hot bread; he was very astonished and told the mother what had happened to their son. From that day on, they knew the grace that was upon their son and they no longer left him to guard the sheep. He learned the books of the Church by heart; his parents asked him to marry, but he did not consent; when he was eighteen years old, he was ordained a priest. Sem'on, his paternal cousin, abandoned the guarding of the sheep and became his disciple; it is he who recounted his miracles, for God, through his intermediary, produced great miracles; all those who were sick with any illness whatsoever were brought to him, he prayed over the oil with which he anointed them, and they were healed of their illnesses. - He gave them admonitions and said to them: "The many illnesses that afflict you are because of your sins." This righteous man performed countless miracles: Here is one of his miracles; a soldier had taken a load of barley from a widow; she came to find the saint and lodged a complaint against the soldier: the saint cursed him and his mare died when she ate of this barley. Once, the chief of the emperor's soldiers had come to collect taxes; he had a servant who was blind in one eye; this servant came to find Saint Yohannes and received his blessing: immediately the one of his eyes

that was blind opened and he saw well with that eye. This saint saw in the Holy Spirit the actions of all men; he made their sins known to them and rebuked them for them. The fame of this saint reached the ears of a king named Maryanos; the latter had an only daughter: a serpent had entered her belly; her belly had swollen and she was on the point of dying: her father had already given many goods to the physicians without them having been able to heal her, when the chief of the soldiers made known to him the story of what Saint Yohannes had done, and the manner in which he had opened the eye of his servant. The king wanted to send someone to bring the saint; but the latter knew in his spirit that the king wanted to bring him to him. As he feared the fatigues of the journey and the sea, a cloud carried him immediately from the city of Sermalas until it led him into the city of Antioch and made him stand at (the bedside of) the king's bed; seeing this, the king was seized with terror and fear. The Saint gave him explanations: "I am," he said to him, "the poor man to whom you wanted to send someone, in order to bring me to you." The king brought his daughter to Saint Yohannes; the latter prayed over her and the serpent came out of her belly, without having caused her damage or done her harm. The king and all the people of the royal household asked the Saint for his blessing; they were

in great astonishment and praised God, who gives such grace to those who love Him and who practice His commandments. The king offered him goods and gifts in great number, but he did not accept the slightest thing. The king wanted to keep him near him, but that was not possible for him. He held him by his belt, in order to prevent him from leaving, but a cloud carried him away, while the king held the belt between his hands; the belt tore in the king's hands: Saint Yohannes arrived in his city that night. The king built a church over the place where the belt tore; it has been called, until this day, the Church of the Belt. When Saint Yohannes celebrated the Eucharistic sacrifice, he saw the good and the wicked, those who were worthy to receive the Eucharist and those who were unworthy. When Diocletian became unfaithful and worshipped idols, this Saint took with him Sem'on his paternal cousin; they went to the city of Alexandria and confessed Our Lord Jesus Christ before the judge. The latter inflicted great tortures upon them. After which they were beheaded with the sword, and their souls went into eternal rest. Their bodies, until now, remained in the city of Gamnudi, great
ስ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወኩነኖሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወአምድኅረዝ ፣ መተ”ሩ ፡ አርእ ስቲሆሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወሖረ ፣ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ዕረፍት ፣ ዘለዓለም ፡ ወሥጋሆ ሙ ፤ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ገምኑዲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ እምሥጋሆሙ ፣ ተአምራ ት ፡ ወፈውስ ፣ ዓበይት ። እግዚአብሔር ፣ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ም
wonders and healings occurring through their bodies. May God, through the prayer of John, have mercy on us, and may his blessing be with us! Amen.

On this day also, memory of the great saint Abba Isayyas (Isaiah), anchorite in the desert of Scetis. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us! Amen.

faithful and blessed parents who walked in the way of God, acting according to His will and according to His commandments. When they had brought him into the world, they raised him in the fear of God. One day, holy monks came to him: among them, there was a holy and righteous old man, leading a beautiful ascetic life: he had had a beautiful vision: many bishops were gathering around the child, laying their hands on him and blessing him; all were saying: "Amen, amen." When this old man had awakened from his vision, he reflected and said: "This child will be a great personage." He told his father to have him instructed with care, for he was going to become the head and the leader of many peoples. "As for you," he said to him, "you will not see it then, it will be after your death." He told him his vision and praised God, saying: "What then will this child be?"

When he was two years old, his father died surrounded by the mercy of Our Lord Jesus Christ. The child's maternal uncle, named Abba Petros (Peter), bishop of the city of Behna, was living. He was admirable for his asceticism. He knew the divine Scripture entirely, the Old and the New Testament; he received the child and instructed him. All those who saw him admired the grace of God that was in him, his intelligence

ም”ሮቶ ፡ወተሰይመ ፡ዲያቆነ ፡፡ ወዕበ፣፡ ኮነ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ፲ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ይልበስ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፣ ወመነነ ፡ ፍቅረ ፣ ዓለም ፣ ኃላፊ ። ወመጠዎ ፣ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆዕስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፣ ለ፳ብአሲ ፡ መነኮስ ፣፡ ቡሩክ ። ዘስሙ ፡ አባ ፣ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ቀሲስ ። ወኮነ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ጽሙደ ፡ ወመስተጋድለ ፡ በአምልኮ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወነሥኦ ፡ ለሕፃን ፡ ወመሀሮ ፣ ገድለ ፡ ም”ንኩስና ፡ በሕዳጥ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ተአምራት ፣ወመን ክራት ፡ ወትሩፋት ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ወብኹኃን ፡፣ ሰብእ ፡፣ ወደስዎ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ተጋድሎቱ ፡ ወተጸምዶቱ ፡ ወትሕትናሁ ።
and his knowledge; he was ordained a deacon. When he was twelve years old, he wanted to put on the monastic habit and despised the love of this perishable world (O, A: this world). The bishop Abba Petros (Peter) placed him in the hands of an excellent monk by the name of Abba Petros (Peter), a priest, devout, valiant in the practice of a beautiful worship of God, who took the child and instructed him for some days in monastic asceticism: through him, through this child, miracles, wonders, and beautiful virtues were produced; many praised him for his asceticism, his devotion, and his humility.
ወእምድኅረ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ አባ ፣ ጴጥሮስ ፣ መምህሩ ፣ ፈለሰ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ወነበረ ፣ ህየ ፣ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትጋደል ፡ ወይጸመ ድ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ በትጋህ ፡ ወበሰጊድ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጽርዓት ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ ሖ ረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባበ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ለ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ ዘእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፣ በደብረ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ማርሞስ ፡ ወተጋደለ፣ ወሐደሰ ፡ ሕንጸታ ፡ፍ ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ሀገረ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወተባረከ ፡ እመካናት ፡ ቅዱሳት ። ወህሀየ ፡ ተሰይመ ፡ ቀሲሊሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ ዘትንሣኤ ፣ ቅድስት ፣0 እምእዴሁ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ወእምህየ ፡ ተመይ
When the priest Abba Petros, his master, died, he went into the monastery of Saint Anthony, where he remained for many years, giving himself to asceticism and serving God without interruption through fasting, prayer, vigils, and adoration. After he had been in the desert of Scetis, at the convent of Saint Abba Macarius, he became superior of the church of Our Lady Mary at the convent of Saint Marmos; he practiced asceticism, and rebuilt this convent. Then, he went to the city of Jerusalem, to be blessed by the visit to the Holy Places; there, he was established as priest of the Church of the holy Resurrection (the Anastasis), by Saint Abba Mika'el (Michael), patriarch (O: metropolitan); from there, he returned to the city of Misr (Old Cairo); he remained in the Church of Ma'alga


[p. 313] {#po.7.p313}
ጠ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ምስር ።፡ ወነበረ ፡ በቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘመዐልቃ ፡ ዘእግዝእትነ ፡ ማ ርያም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጽሕፍ ፣ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ቅዱሳተ ፡ ዘቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ ቅድስት ፡፡
dedicated to Our Lady Mary, transcribing the Sacred Scriptures of the Holy Church.
ወበውእቱ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ኅዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ ።፡ ወተጋብኡ ፡ ኤሏ ስ ፣ ቆዕሳት ፡ ወልሂቃን ፡ ወሕዝብ ፡ መሃይምናን ፣ ወተሰነአው ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ በ8ቃል፣ ወበ፳ምክር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚምዎ ፡ ወሰይጣንሰ ፡ ጸሳኤ ፡ሠናያት ፣ አንሥአ ፡ ሰብአ ፣ እኩ ያነ ፡ ወቀንኡ ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወኢፈቀዱ ፡ ሚመቶ ፣ አላ ፡ ጫምዎ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መንፈ ሳዊ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘፅውቅ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አክሊል ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወሐዊር ፡ ርቱዕ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ነውር ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ አባ ፡ አት ናቴዎስ ፡ ሐዋርያዊ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አፅረፈ ፡፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፣ ወል ሂቃን ፣ ወሕዝብ ፡ ወጸሐፉ ፡ በእደዊሆሙ ፡ ወሠምሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰየም ፡ አባ ፡፣ ገብርኤ ል ፡ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወክዕበ ፣ አንሥአ ፡ ጸላኤ ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ሁከተ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ሕዝብ ፣ ወተሰነአው ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጽሐሩ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ወስመ ፡ ካልአን ፡ በክርታስ ፡ ያንብርዎሙ ፡፣ ዲ በ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ፡፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወይጸልዩ ፣ ወይቀድሱ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ያመዓልተ ፣ ወዘኃረ ዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ሚመት ፡ ክብርት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ስሞ ፡ ወገብሩ፣
At that moment, the father patriarch Qerellos (Cyril) died and, for some days, there was no patriarch on the seat of Mark the Evangelist. The bishops, the notables, and the people of the faithful gathered. All with one accord and with one voice were unanimous in deciding that he should be established as patriarch; but Satan, enemy of the good, caused wicked men to arise who envied him and who did not want him to be consecrated; but they established as patriarch the holy and devout father Abba Atnatewos (Athanasius), known by the name of Walda Aklil (son of the crown; O, A: Kelil). He gave the example of a beautiful conduct and walked in the straight path, without failing, in the manner of Saint Athanasius the Apostolic; then he died. Again, the bishops, the notables, and the people gathered; they wrote with their own hand and were in agreement that Abba Gabr'el should be established as patriarch. Again, the enemy of the good, the devil, caused dissension to break out among the people; they agreed to write on pieces of paper the name of Gabr'el and the names of the other candidates, to deposit these with the name of Gabr'el on the altar, to pray and to offer the sacrifice concerning their election for three days, praying that God would manifest the name of the one He had chosen for this illustrious office. They
